Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a divisors algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 960. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 252/3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 960 + O(1/n^252). Summing over all modes and applying the divisors convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
